# PropFirmMath — account master report

Snapshot: 2026-11-28. 151 firms; 1271 unique account records. 265 marked verified, 936 partial, 70 starting. Prices are recorded values, not checkout quotes. Regional eligibility and complete program verification remain incomplete.
